class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eChangelog\u003c/h1\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-changelog\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Changelog\" href=\"#changelog\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e2026/06/18 3.4 Released\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis release focuses on \u003cstrong\u003eOCR capability upgrades for the pipeline backend\u003c/strong\u003e, \u003cstrong\u003eOCR processing pipeline optimization\u003c/strong\u003e, and \u003cstrong\u003emodel download experience improvements\u003c/strong\u003e. The main updates include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOCR model upgrade and processing acceleration\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe OCR model for the \u003ccode\u003epipeline\u003c/code\u003e backend has been upgraded to \u003ccode\u003ePP-OCRv6\u003c/code\u003e, improving OCR accuracy by about \u003ccode\u003e11%\u003c/code\u003e on OmniDocBench v1.6.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemoved Japanese, Traditional Chinese, English, and Latin options from OCR language selection. These scenarios are now routed to the \u003ccode\u003ech\u003c/code\u003e OCR model, simplifying model configuration and language selection.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOptimized the OCR inference and processing pipeline, increasing OCR processing speed by about \u003ccode\u003e100%\u003c/code\u003e and significantly improving parsing efficiency for batch documents and OCR-intensive documents.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel download logic optimization\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdded automatic model source selection, allowing first-time installations to choose a better model source based on the current network environment.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore downloading models, MinerU now prioritizes checking locally downloaded model cache files. Cache hits can be reused directly, reducing repeated downloads and unnecessary remote requests.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor more details about model source configuration, automatic source selection, and local model usage, see the \u003ca href=\"https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/usage/model_source/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eModel Source Documentation\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith the 3.4 release, MinerU further improves the parsing accuracy and processing efficiency of the \u003ccode\u003epipeline\u003c/code\u003e backend in OCR scenarios. It also optimizes model downloads, cache reuse, and local configuration write-back, making first-time installation, model updates, and multi-environment deployment more stable and automated.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e2026/06/11 3.3 Released\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis release focuses on \u003cstrong\u003eHybrid parsing performance optimization\u003c/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003eVLM model capability upgrades\u003c/strong\u003e. The main updates include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNew \u003ccode\u003eeffort\u003c/code\u003e parsing-strength parameter for the Hybrid backend\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdded two parsing-strength levels, \u003ccode\u003emedium\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003ehigh\u003c/code\u003e, allowing users to balance parsing speed, parsing accuracy, and feature requirements.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOn OmniDocBench v1.6, \u003ccode\u003emedium\u003c/code\u003e reduces overall accuracy by only \u003ccode\u003e0.13\u003c/code\u003e points compared with \u003ccode\u003ehigh\u003c/code\u003e, while delivering \u003ccode\u003e35%\u003c/code\u003e ~ \u003ccode\u003e220%\u003c/code\u003e parsing speed improvements across different devices and scenarios:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLinux: about \u003ccode\u003e80%\u003c/code\u003e faster for text PDF scenarios and about \u003ccode\u003e35%\u003c/code\u003e faster for OCR scenarios\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWindows: about \u003ccode\u003e90%\u003c/code\u003e faster for text PDF scenarios and about \u003ccode\u003e45%\u003c/code\u003e faster for OCR scenarios\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emacOS: about \u003ccode\u003e220%\u003c/code\u003e faster for text PDF scenarios and about \u003ccode\u003e50%\u003c/code\u003e faster for OCR scenarios\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe default Hybrid backend now uses \u003ccode\u003eeffort=medium\u003c/code\u003e, significantly improving overall parsing efficiency while maintaining high parsing accuracy.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003emedium\u003c/code\u003e level does not support \u003ccode\u003eimage analysis\u003c/code\u003e; for maximum parsing accuracy or \u003ccode\u003eimage analysis\u003c/code\u003e support, switch to the high-strength parsing mode with \u003ccode\u003eeffort=high\u003c/code\u003e, which may have an impact on parsing speed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eVLM model upgraded to \u003ccode\u003eMinerU2.5-Pro-2605-1.2B\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFixed multiple model issues found in the \u003ccode\u003e2604\u003c/code\u003e version, further improving parsing stability on complex documents.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdded native multilingual OCR support, reducing the need for extra language-parameter configuration and improving out-of-the-box usability for multilingual documents.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith the 3.3 release, MinerU further improves Hybrid backend efficiency across platforms and scenarios while maintaining high-accuracy parsing. The default \u003ccode\u003emedium\u003c/code\u003e effort level is better suited for most day-to-day document processing tasks, while \u003ccode\u003ehigh\u003c/code\u003e is designed for scenarios that require maximum parsing accuracy or \u003ccode\u003eimage analysis\u003c/code\u003e capabilities.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e2026/04/18 3.1.0 Released\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis release focuses on \u003cstrong\u003elicensing openness, parsing accuracy, and full-format native support\u003c/strong\u003e. The main updates include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLicense upgrade\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMinerU has officially moved from \u003ccode\u003eAGPLv3\u003c/code\u003e to the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/blob/master/LICENSE.md\"\u003eMinerU Open Source License\u003c/a\u003e, a custom license based on \u003ccode\u003eApache 2.0\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis change significantly reduces adoption friction for both community users and commercial deployments, making MinerU easier to integrate into real-world workflows.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVLM main model upgrade\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe primary VLM model has been upgraded to \u003ccode\u003eMinerU2.5-Pro-2604-1.2B\u003c/code\u003e, bringing overall parsing accuracy to a state-of-the-art level.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe new model now supports image and chart parsing, truncated paragraph merging, cross-page table merging, and image recognition inside tables, further strengthening performance on complex document layouts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFull-format native parsing support\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNative parsing support has now been extended to \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMinerU now fully supports parsing across images, \u003ccode\u003ePDF\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e, providing a more complete multi-format document understanding workflow.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith the 3.1.0 release, MinerU becomes more open, more accurate, and easier to adopt in production. The new license lowers the barrier for both community and commercial use, \u003ccode\u003eMinerU2.5-Pro-2604-1.2B\u003c/code\u003e improves parsing quality on complex content, and native \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e support completes end-to-end coverage of mainstream document formats.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e2026/03/29 3.0.0 Released\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis release delivers a systematic upgrade centered on \u003cstrong\u003eparsing capability, system architecture, and engineering usability\u003c/strong\u003e. The main updates include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNative \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e parsing\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOfficial support for native \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e parsing, delivering high-precision results without hallucinations.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompared with the traditional workflow of first converting \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003ePDF\u003c/code\u003e and then parsing it, end-to-end speed is improved by tens of times, making it better suited for scenarios with high requirements for both accuracy and throughput.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epipeline\u003c/code\u003e backend upgrade\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003epipeline\u003c/code\u003e backend achieves a score of \u003ccode\u003e86.2\u003c/code\u003e on OmniDocBench (v1.5), surpassing the accuracy of the previous-generation mainstream VLM \u003ccode\u003eMinerU2.0-2505-0.9B\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdded support for parsing images/formulas inside tables, seal text recognition, vertical text support, and interline formula numbering recognition, continuously improving parsing quality for complex document scenarios.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhile maintaining high accuracy, it keeps resource usage extremely low and continues to support inference in pure CPU environments.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAPI / CLI / Router\u003c/code\u003e orchestration upgrade\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emineru\u003c/code\u003e now runs as an orchestration client based on \u003ccode\u003emineru-api\u003c/code\u003e; when \u003ccode\u003e--api-url\u003c/code\u003e is not provided, it will automatically start a local temporary service.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emineru-api\u003c/code\u003e adds a new asynchronous task endpoint \u003ccode\u003ePOST /tasks\u003c/code\u003e, supporting task submission, status querying, and result retrieval; meanwhile, it retains the synchronous parsing endpoint \u003ccode\u003ePOST /file_parse\u003c/code\u003e for compatibility with legacy plugins.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdded \u003ccode\u003emineru-router\u003c/code\u003e, designed for unified entry deployment and task routing across multiple services and multiple GPUs; its interfaces are fully compatible with \u003ccode\u003emineru-api\u003c/code\u003e and support automatic task load balancing.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDeployment and usability improvements\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eResolved compatibility issues with \u003ccode\u003etorch \u0026gt;= 2.8\u003c/code\u003e; the base image has been upgraded to \u003ccode\u003evllm0.11.2 + torch2.9.0\u003c/code\u003e, unifying installation paths across different Compute Capabilities.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOptimized the parsing pipeline with a sliding-window mechanism, significantly reducing peak memory usage in long-document scenarios, so documents with tens of thousands of pages no longer need to be split manually.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBatch inference in \u003ccode\u003epipeline\u003c/code\u003e now supports streaming writes to disk, allowing completed parsing results to be written out in time and further improving the experience for long-running tasks.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompleted thread-safety optimization and now fully supports multi-threaded concurrent inference; together with \u003ccode\u003emineru-router\u003c/code\u003e, this enables one-click multi-GPU deployment and makes it easy to build high-concurrency, high-throughput parsing systems.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompletely removed the use of two AGPLv3 models (\u003ccode\u003edoclayoutyolo\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003emfd_yolov8\u003c/code\u003e) and one CC-BY-NC-SA 4.0 model (\u003ccode\u003elayoutreader\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis update is not just a set of feature enhancements, but a key leap forward in MinerU's overall system capabilities. We specifically addressed the peak memory usage issue in long-document parsing. Through optimizations such as sliding windows and streaming writes to disk, ultra-long document parsing has moved from “requiring manual splitting and careful handling” to being “stable, scalable, and ready for production workloads.” At the same time, we completed thread-safety optimization and fully enabled multi-threaded concurrent inference, further improving single-machine resource utilization and runtime stability under high-concurrency workloads. On top of this, with \u003ccode\u003emineru-router\u003c/code\u003e and the new \u003ccode\u003eAPI / CLI\u003c/code\u003e orchestration framework, MinerU now supports one-click multi-GPU deployment, unified access across multiple services, and automatic task load balancing, significantly reducing the difficulty of large-scale deployment. As a result, MinerU is evolving from a standalone data production tool into a large-scale document parsing foundation for high-concurrency and high-throughput scenarios, providing enterprise-grade document data processing with infrastructure that is more stable, more efficient, and easier to scale.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e📝 View the complete \u003ca href=\"https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/reference/changelog/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eChangelog\u003c/a\u003e for more historical version information\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMinerU is a document parsing tool that converts \u003ccode\u003ePDF\u003c/code\u003e, image, \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e inputs into machine-readable formats such as Markdown and JSON for downstream retrieval, extraction, and processing.\nMinerU was born during the pre-training process of \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/InternLM/InternLM\"\u003eInternLM\u003c/a\u003e. We focus on solving symbol conversion issues in scientific literature and hope to contribute to technological development in the era of large models.\nCompared to well-known commercial products, MinerU is still young. If you encounter any issues or if the results are not as expected, please submit an issue on \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/issues\"\u003eissue\u003c/a\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003eattach the relevant document or sample file\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdetails open=\"\" class=\"details-reset border rounded-2\"\u003e\n  \u003csummary Features\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-key-features\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Key Features\" href=\"#key-features\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSupport \u003ccode\u003ePDF\u003c/code\u003e, image, \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e inputs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemove headers, footers, footnotes, page numbers, etc., to ensure semantic coherence.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOutput text in human-readable order, suitable for single-column, multi-column, and complex layouts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePreserve the structure of the original document, including headings, paragraphs, lists, etc.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExtract images, image descriptions, tables, table titles, and footnotes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatically recognize and convert formulas in the document to LaTeX format.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatically recognize and convert tables in the document to HTML format.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatically detect scanned PDFs and garbled PDFs and enable OCR functionality.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOCR supports detection and recognition of 109 languages.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSupports multiple output formats, such as multimodal and NLP Markdown, JSON sorted by reading order, and rich intermediate formats.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSupports various visualization results, including layout visualization and span visualization, for efficient confirmation of output quality.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBuilt-in CLI, FastAPI, Gradio WebUI, for local orchestration and multi-service deployment.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSupports running in a pure CPU environment, and also supports GPU/MPS acceleration\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompatible with Windows, Linux, and Mac platforms.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick In scenarios such as complex layouts, scanned pages, and handwritten content, the parsing results may fall short of expectations. We recommend trying the online demo first to evaluate MinerU's parsing quality and suitability before choosing an appropriate deployment method based on your actual needs.\nIf you have \u003cstrong\u003edocument\u003c/strong\u003e samples with unsatisfactory parsing results, feel free to share them in an \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/issues\"\u003eissue\u003c/a\u003e. We will continue improving the parsing capabilities.\nIf you encounter any installation issues, please first consult the \u003ca href=\"#faq\"\u003eFAQ\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv 0Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003eWarning\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePre-installation Notice—Hardware and Software Environment Support\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo ensure the stability and reliability of the project, we only optimize and test for specific hardware and software environments during development. This ensures that users deploying and running the project on recommended system configurations will get the best performance with the fewest compatibility issues.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy focusing resources on the mainline environment, our team can more efficiently resolve potential bugs and develop new features.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn non-mainline environments, due to the diversity of hardware and software configurations, as well as third-party dependency compatibility issues, we cannot guarantee 100% project availability. Therefore, for users who wish to use this project in non-recommended environments, we suggest carefully reading the documentation and FAQ first. Most issues already have corresponding solutions in the FAQ. We also encourage community feedback to help us gradually expand support.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n  \u003cthead\u003e\n colspan=\"2\"\u003e✅\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n        \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eGPU Acceleration\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"4\"\u003eVolta and later architecture GPUs or Apple Silicon\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd rowspan=\"2\"\u003eNot Required\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eMin VRAM\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003e4GB\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"2\"\u003e8GB\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003e2GB\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eRAM\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"3\"\u003eMin 16GB, Recommended 32GB or more\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"2\"\u003eMin 16GB\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eDisk Space\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"3\"\u003eMin 20GB, SSD Recommended\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"2\"\u003eMin 2GB\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003ePython Version\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003ctd colspan=\"5\"\u003e3.10-3.13\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003csup\u003e1\u003c/sup\u003e Accuracy metrics are the End-to-End Evaluation Overall scores from OmniDocBench (v1.6), based on the latest version of \u003ccode\u003eMinerU\u003c/code\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003csup\u003e2\u003c/sup\u003e Servers compatible with OpenAI API, such as local model servers or remote model services deployed via inference frameworks like \u003ccode\u003evLLM\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eSGLang\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eLMDeploy\u003c/code\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003csup\u003e3\u003c/sup\u003e Linux only supports distributions from 2019 and later.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003csup\u003e4\u003c/sup\u003e Since the key dependency \u003ccode\u003eray\u003c/code\u003e does not support Python 3.13 on Windows, only versions 3.10~3.12 are supported.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003csup\u003e5\u003c/sup\u003e macOS requires version 14.0 or later.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e -b pipeline\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003emineru\u003c/code\u003e currently supports local \u003ccode\u003ePDF\u003c/code\u003e, image, \u003ccode\u003eDOCX\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePPTX\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003eXLSX\u003c/code\u003e file or directory inputs, and can be used for document parsing through the CLI, API, WebUI, and \u003ccode\u003emineru-router\u003c/code\u003e. For detailed instructions, please refer to the \u003ca href=\"https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/usage/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eUsage Guide\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv <details>
<summary>MinerU — High-accuracy document parsing engine for LLM · RAG · Agent workflows</summary>
Converts PDF · DOCX · PPTX · XLSX · Images · Web pages into structured Markdown / JSON · VLM+OCR dual engine · 109 languages <br>
MCP Server · LangChain / Dify / FastGPT native integration · 10+ domestic AI chip support
<p dir="auto"><strong>🔍 Core Parsing Capabilities</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Native support for <code>DOCX</code>, <code>PPTX</code>, and <code>XLSX</code> parsing</li>
<li>Formulas → LaTeX · Tables → HTML, accurate layout reconstruction</li>
<li>Supports scanned docs, handwriting, multi-column layouts, cross-page table merging</li>
<li>Output follows human reading order with automatic header/footer removal</li>
<li>VLM + OCR dual engine, 109-language OCR recognition</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>🔌 Integration</strong></p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Changelog</h1><a id="user-content-changelog" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Changelog" href="#changelog"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">2026/06/18 3.4 Released</p>
<p dir="auto">This release focuses on <strong>OCR capability upgrades for the pipeline backend</strong>, <strong>OCR processing pipeline optimization</strong>, and <strong>model download experience improvements</strong>. The main updates include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">OCR model upgrade and processing acceleration</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The OCR model for the <code>pipeline</code> backend has been upgraded to <code>PP-OCRv6</code>, improving OCR accuracy by about <code>11%</code> on OmniDocBench v1.6.</li>
<li>Removed Japanese, Traditional Chinese, English, and Latin options from OCR language selection. These scenarios are now routed to the <code>ch</code> OCR model, simplifying model configuration and language selection.</li>
<li>Optimized the OCR inference and processing pipeline, increasing OCR processing speed by about <code>100%</code> and significantly improving parsing efficiency for batch documents and OCR-intensive documents.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Model download logic optimization</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Added automatic model source selection, allowing first-time installations to choose a better model source based on the current network environment.</li>
<li>Before downloading models, MinerU now prioritizes checking locally downloaded model cache files. Cache hits can be reused directly, reducing repeated downloads and unnecessary remote requests.</li>
<li>For more details about model source configuration, automatic source selection, and local model usage, see the <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/usage/model_source/" rel="nofollow">Model Source Documentation</a>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">With the 3.4 release, MinerU further improves the parsing accuracy and processing efficiency of the <code>pipeline</code> backend in OCR scenarios. It also optimizes model downloads, cache reuse, and local configuration write-back, making first-time installation, model updates, and multi-environment deployment more stable and automated.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">2026/06/11 3.3 Released</p>
<p dir="auto">This release focuses on <strong>Hybrid parsing performance optimization</strong> and <strong>VLM model capability upgrades</strong>. The main updates include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">New <code>effort</code> parsing-strength parameter for the Hybrid backend</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Added two parsing-strength levels, <code>medium</code> and <code>high</code>, allowing users to balance parsing speed, parsing accuracy, and feature requirements.</li>
<li>On OmniDocBench v1.6, <code>medium</code> reduces overall accuracy by only <code>0.13</code> points compared with <code>high</code>, while delivering <code>35%</code> ~ <code>220%</code> parsing speed improvements across different devices and scenarios:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Linux: about <code>80%</code> faster for text PDF scenarios and about <code>35%</code> faster for OCR scenarios</li>
<li>Windows: about <code>90%</code> faster for text PDF scenarios and about <code>45%</code> faster for OCR scenarios</li>
<li>macOS: about <code>220%</code> faster for text PDF scenarios and about <code>50%</code> faster for OCR scenarios</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>The default Hybrid backend now uses <code>effort=medium</code>, significantly improving overall parsing efficiency while maintaining high parsing accuracy.</li>
<li>The <code>medium</code> level does not support <code>image analysis</code>; for maximum parsing accuracy or <code>image analysis</code> support, switch to the high-strength parsing mode with <code>effort=high</code>, which may have an impact on parsing speed.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">VLM model upgraded to <code>MinerU2.5-Pro-2605-1.2B</code></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Fixed multiple model issues found in the <code>2604</code> version, further improving parsing stability on complex documents.</li>
<li>Added native multilingual OCR support, reducing the need for extra language-parameter configuration and improving out-of-the-box usability for multilingual documents.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">With the 3.3 release, MinerU further improves Hybrid backend efficiency across platforms and scenarios while maintaining high-accuracy parsing. The default <code>medium</code> effort level is better suited for most day-to-day document processing tasks, while <code>high</code> is designed for scenarios that require maximum parsing accuracy or <code>image analysis</code> capabilities.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">2026/04/18 3.1.0 Released</p>
<p dir="auto">This release focuses on <strong>licensing openness, parsing accuracy, and full-format native support</strong>. The main updates include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>License upgrade
<ul dir="auto">
<li>MinerU has officially moved from <code>AGPLv3</code> to the <a href="https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/blob/master/LICENSE.md">MinerU Open Source License</a>, a custom license based on <code>Apache 2.0</code>.</li>
<li>This change significantly reduces adoption friction for both community users and commercial deployments, making MinerU easier to integrate into real-world workflows.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>VLM main model upgrade
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The primary VLM model has been upgraded to <code>MinerU2.5-Pro-2604-1.2B</code>, bringing overall parsing accuracy to a state-of-the-art level.</li>
<li>The new model now supports image and chart parsing, truncated paragraph merging, cross-page table merging, and image recognition inside tables, further strengthening performance on complex document layouts.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Full-format native parsing support
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Native parsing support has now been extended to <code>PPTX</code> and <code>XLSX</code>.</li>
<li>MinerU now fully supports parsing across images, <code>PDF</code>, <code>DOCX</code>, <code>PPTX</code>, and <code>XLSX</code>, providing a more complete multi-format document understanding workflow.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">With the 3.1.0 release, MinerU becomes more open, more accurate, and easier to adopt in production. The new license lowers the barrier for both community and commercial use, <code>MinerU2.5-Pro-2604-1.2B</code> improves parsing quality on complex content, and native <code>PPTX</code> / <code>XLSX</code> support completes end-to-end coverage of mainstream document formats.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">2026/03/29 3.0.0 Released</p>
<p dir="auto">This release delivers a systematic upgrade centered on <strong>parsing capability, system architecture, and engineering usability</strong>. The main updates include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Native <code>DOCX</code> parsing
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Official support for native <code>DOCX</code> parsing, delivering high-precision results without hallucinations.</li>
<li>Compared with the traditional workflow of first converting <code>DOCX</code> to <code>PDF</code> and then parsing it, end-to-end speed is improved by tens of times, making it better suited for scenarios with high requirements for both accuracy and throughput.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>pipeline</code> backend upgrade
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The <code>pipeline</code> backend achieves a score of <code>86.2</code> on OmniDocBench (v1.5), surpassing the accuracy of the previous-generation mainstream VLM <code>MinerU2.0-2505-0.9B</code>.</li>
<li>Added support for parsing images/formulas inside tables, seal text recognition, vertical text support, and interline formula numbering recognition, continuously improving parsing quality for complex document scenarios.</li>
<li>While maintaining high accuracy, it keeps resource usage extremely low and continues to support inference in pure CPU environments.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>API / CLI / Router</code> orchestration upgrade
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>mineru</code> now runs as an orchestration client based on <code>mineru-api</code>; when <code>--api-url</code> is not provided, it will automatically start a local temporary service.</li>
<li><code>mineru-api</code> adds a new asynchronous task endpoint <code>POST /tasks</code>, supporting task submission, status querying, and result retrieval; meanwhile, it retains the synchronous parsing endpoint <code>POST /file_parse</code> for compatibility with legacy plugins.</li>
<li>Added <code>mineru-router</code>, designed for unified entry deployment and task routing across multiple services and multiple GPUs; its interfaces are fully compatible with <code>mineru-api</code> and support automatic task load balancing.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Deployment and usability improvements
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Resolved compatibility issues with <code>torch &gt;= 2.8</code>; the base image has been upgraded to <code>vllm0.11.2 + torch2.9.0</code>, unifying installation paths across different Compute Capabilities.</li>
<li>Optimized the parsing pipeline with a sliding-window mechanism, significantly reducing peak memory usage in long-document scenarios, so documents with tens of thousands of pages no longer need to be split manually.</li>
<li>Batch inference in <code>pipeline</code> now supports streaming writes to disk, allowing completed parsing results to be written out in time and further improving the experience for long-running tasks.</li>
<li>Completed thread-safety optimization and now fully supports multi-threaded concurrent inference; together with <code>mineru-router</code>, this enables one-click multi-GPU deployment and makes it easy to build high-concurrency, high-throughput parsing systems.</li>
<li>Completely removed the use of two AGPLv3 models (<code>doclayoutyolo</code> and <code>mfd_yolov8</code>) and one CC-BY-NC-SA 4.0 model (<code>layoutreader</code>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This update is not just a set of feature enhancements, but a key leap forward in MinerU's overall system capabilities. We specifically addressed the peak memory usage issue in long-document parsing. Through optimizations such as sliding windows and streaming writes to disk, ultra-long document parsing has moved from “requiring manual splitting and careful handling” to being “stable, scalable, and ready for production workloads.” At the same time, we completed thread-safety optimization and fully enabled multi-threaded concurrent inference, further improving single-machine resource utilization and runtime stability under high-concurrency workloads. On top of this, with <code>mineru-router</code> and the new <code>API / CLI</code> orchestration framework, MinerU now supports one-click multi-GPU deployment, unified access across multiple services, and automatic task load balancing, significantly reducing the difficulty of large-scale deployment. As a result, MinerU is evolving from a standalone data production tool into a large-scale document parsing foundation for high-concurrency and high-throughput scenarios, providing enterprise-grade document data processing with infrastructure that is more stable, more efficient, and easier to scale.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">📝 View the complete <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/reference/changelog/" rel="nofollow">Changelog</a> for more historical version information</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">MinerU</h1><a id="user-content-mineru" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: MinerU" href="#mineru"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project Introduction</h2><a id="user-content-project-introduction" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project Introduction" href="#project-introduction"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">MinerU is a document parsing tool that converts <code>PDF</code>, image, <code>DOCX</code>, <code>PPTX</code>, and <code>XLSX</code> inputs into machine-readable formats such as Markdown and JSON for downstream retrieval, extraction, and processing.
MinerU was born during the pre-training process of <a href="https://github.com/InternLM/InternLM">InternLM</a>. We focus on solving symbol conversion issues in scientific literature and hope to contribute to technological development in the era of large models.
Compared to well-known commercial products, MinerU is still young. If you encounter any issues or if the results are not as expected, please submit an issue on <a href="https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/issues">issue</a> and <strong>attach the relevant document or sample file</strong>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key Features</h2><a id="user-content-key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key Features" href="#key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Support <code>PDF</code>, image, <code>DOCX</code>, <code>PPTX</code>, and <code>XLSX</code> inputs.</li>
<li>Remove headers, footers, footnotes, page numbers, etc., to ensure semantic coherence.</li>
<li>Output text in human-readable order, suitable for single-column, multi-column, and complex layouts.</li>
<li>Preserve the structure of the original document, including headings, paragraphs, lists, etc.</li>
<li>Extract images, image descriptions, tables, table titles, and footnotes.</li>
<li>Automatically recognize and convert formulas in the document to LaTeX format.</li>
<li>Automatically recognize and convert tables in the document to HTML format.</li>
<li>Automatically detect scanned PDFs and garbled PDFs and enable OCR functionality.</li>
<li>OCR supports detection and recognition of 109 languages.</li>
<li>Supports multiple output formats, such as multimodal and NLP Markdown, JSON sorted by reading order, and rich intermediate formats.</li>
<li>Supports various visualization results, including layout visualization and span visualization, for efficient confirmation of output quality.</li>
<li>Built-in CLI, FastAPI, Gradio WebUI, for local orchestration and multi-service deployment.</li>
<li>Supports running in a pure CPU environment, and also supports GPU/MPS acceleration</li>
<li>Compatible with Windows, Linux, and Mac platforms.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h1><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Document parsing is a difficult and complex task. In scenarios such as complex layouts, scanned pages, and handwritten content, the parsing results may fall short of expectations. We recommend trying the online demo first to evaluate MinerU's parsing quality and suitability before choosing an appropriate deployment method based on your actual needs.
If you have <strong>document</strong> samples with unsatisfactory parsing results, feel free to share them in an <a href="https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/issues">issue</a>. We will continue improving the parsing capabilities.
If you encounter any installation issues, please first consult the <a href="#faq">FAQ</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Online Experience</h2><a id="user-content-online-experience" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Online Experience" href="#online-experience"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Official online web application</h3><a id="user-content-official-online-web-application" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Official online web application" href="#official-online-web-application"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The official online version has the same functionality as the client, with a beautiful interface and rich features, requires login to use</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Local Deployment</h2><a id="user-content-local-deployment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Local Deployment" href="#local-deployment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-warning" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-alert mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p><p dir="auto"><strong>Pre-installation Notice—Hardware and Software Environment Support</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">To ensure the stability and reliability of the project, we only optimize and test for specific hardware and software environments during development. This ensures that users deploying and running the project on recommended system configurations will get the best performance with the fewest compatibility issues.</p>
<p dir="auto">By focusing resources on the mainline environment, our team can more efficiently resolve potential bugs and develop new features.</p>
<p dir="auto">In non-mainline environments, due to the diversity of hardware and software configurations, as well as third-party dependency compatibility issues, we cannot guarantee 100% project availability. Therefore, for users who wish to use this project in non-recommended environments, we suggest carefully reading the documentation and FAQ first. Most issues already have corresponding solutions in the FAQ. We also encourage community feedback to help us gradually expand support.</p>
</div>

<p dir="auto"><sup>1</sup> Accuracy metrics are the End-to-End Evaluation Overall scores from OmniDocBench (v1.6), based on the latest version of <code>MinerU</code>.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Servers compatible with OpenAI API, such as local model servers or remote model services deployed via inference frameworks like <code>vLLM</code>/<code>SGLang</code>/<code>LMDeploy</code>.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Linux only supports distributions from 2019 and later.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Since the key dependency <code>ray</code> does not support Python 3.13 on Windows, only versions 3.10~3.12 are supported.<br>
<sup>5</sup> macOS requires version 14.0 or later.</p>

uv pip install -U <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>mineru[all]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install MinerU from source code</h4><a id="user-content-install-mineru-from-source-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install MinerU from source code" href="#install-mineru-from-source-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU.git
cd MinerU
uv pip install -e .[all]"><pre>git clone https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> MinerU
uv pip install -e .[all]</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>mineru[all]</code> includes all core features, compatible with Windows / Linux / macOS systems, suitable for most users.</li>
<li>If CUDA acceleration is unavailable after installing on Windows, see the <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/faq/#windows-cuda-acceleration" rel="nofollow">Windows CUDA acceleration FAQ</a>.</li>
<li>If you need to specify the inference framework for the VLM model, or only intend to install a lightweight client on an edge device, please refer to the documentation <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/quick_start/extension_modules/" rel="nofollow">Extension Modules Installation Guide</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deploy MinerU using Docker</h4><a id="user-content-deploy-mineru-using-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deploy MinerU using Docker" href="#deploy-mineru-using-docker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">MinerU provides a convenient Docker deployment method, which helps quickly set up the environment and solve some tricky environment compatibility issues.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Docker deployment is only supported on Linux and Windows environments with WSL2 support;</li>
<li>macOS users should refer to the two installation methods above for installation instead of using Docker deployment.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<p dir="auto">You can get the <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/quick_start/docker_deployment/" rel="nofollow">Docker Deployment Instructions</a> in the documentation.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using MinerU</h3><a id="user-content-using-mineru" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using MinerU" href="#using-mineru"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If your device meets the GPU acceleration requirements in the table above, you can use a simple command line for document parsing:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mineru -p &lt;input_path&gt; -o &lt;output_path&gt;"><pre>mineru -p <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>input_path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> -o <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>output_path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If your device does not meet the GPU acceleration requirements, you can specify the backend as <code>pipeline</code> to run in a pure CPU environment:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mineru -p &lt;input_path&gt; -o &lt;output_path&gt; -b pipeline"><pre>mineru -p <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>input_path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> -o <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>output_path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> -b pipeline</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>mineru</code> currently supports local <code>PDF</code>, image, <code>DOCX</code>, <code>PPTX</code>, and <code>XLSX</code> file or directory inputs, and can be used for document parsing through the CLI, API, WebUI, and <code>mineru-router</code>. For detailed instructions, please refer to the <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/usage/" rel="nofollow">Usage Guide</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FAQ</h1><a id="user-content-faq" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FAQ" href="#faq"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>If you encounter any issues during usage, you can first check the <a href="https://opendatalab.github.io/MinerU/faq/" rel="nofollow">FAQ</a> for solutions.</li>
<li>If your issue remains unresolved, you may also use <a href="https://deepwiki.com/opendatalab/MinerU" rel="nofollow">DeepWiki</a> to interact with an AI assistant, which can address most common problems.</li>
<li>If you still cannot resolve the issue, you are welcome to join our community via <a href="https://discord.gg/Tdedn9GTXq" rel="nofollow">Discord</a> or <a href="https://mineru.net/community-portal/?aliasId=3c430f94" rel="nofollow">WeChat</a> to discuss with other users and developers.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">All Thanks To Our Contributors</h1><a id="user-content-all-thanks-to-our-contributors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: All Thanks To Our Contributors" href="#all-thanks-to-our-contributors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
